### 32 — settings
- File: src/services/employer.services.js; src/utils/save.status.change.log.utils.js; src/repository/contract.invoice.repository.js; src/repository/contract.insurance.repository.js; src/repository/contract.invoice.log.repository.js; src/repository/contract.insurance.log.repository.js; src/models/contract.invoice.log.model.js; src/models/contract.insurance.log.model.js
- REQ-ID: SR-03 / CMATRIX §3 (P-12, finding S-3)
- Change: `changeOtherCsStaff` (invoice / insurance CS change) now writes the owner update and its Activity log in one transaction (`request.sequelize.transaction`). The log helper gets optional `{ transaction, throwOnError }`; the 4 repository functions get an optional `options` argument; all defaults leave every other caller unchanged. Also: a record with **no previous CS** no longer crashes on `oldCSStaffDetails.first_name` (logged as "-"), and both log models declare `created_by`, which the tables have but Sequelize was silently dropping.
- Reason: Before, the update was saved first and the log separately; log failures were swallowed, and assigning a CS to an unassigned invoice/insurance threw after the update was already saved. CS-change log rows were stored with `created_by = NULL` (4 such rows seen in staging). Tested on the staging copy in rolled-back transactions: normal change saves both with the real actor; forced log failure rolls the owner change back; an existing no-options call behaves as before.
- Status: Done (node --check OK on all 8 files). **S-2 (contract-linked Insurance re-own) NOT applied**: waiting on your answer whether Insurance CS is managed separately, like Invoice CS.

Helper `recordVisaFieldCorrection` resolves the latest issuance (locked), creates version 1 on first touch of a released visa, and appends a correction when a field's effective value changes. Hooked, BEFORE the projection update and in the same transaction, into the Processing-phase Visa Expiry edit (phase "processing") and End of Processing's E-Visa Payment Date change (phase "end_of_processing", fixed reason "Recorded at End of Processing" per D-6). New read-only `GET /api/processing/visa-issuance-history/:contract_id` (versions, base values, corrections, effective values, current). processing_visa_documents stays the projection; processing_visa_expiry_history is unchanged.
- Reason: EXTEND §3.2–3.7. Unit tests 8/8 (seq ordering, derived release date, determinism, no mutation). Rolled-back integration tests on the staging-schema copy: two corrections (seq 1, 2; old value = previous effective); not released → nothing; first touch → v1 with pre-edit values + verified provenance; unchanged → nothing; blank reason → rollback.
- Status: Done (tsc: no new errors)

- Change: Following the user's suggestion, S5 now reuses `tasks.status` instead of adding `task_status`: 1 = outstanding, 0 = cancelled (old deleted rows), 2 = completed; `deleted_at` / `deleted_by` double as cancelled at / by; only `completed_at` is added. The API still returns `task_status` as text (spec question 6 allows this). Checked every use of `tasks.status` in hrms: delete sets 0; edit/view/calendar read status = 1 (must widen to all states, as A4 requires); the notification list returns the task's status (will add task_status text alongside, so a completed task's notification is not read as deleted). Not used by processing, contract or settings.

### 45 — DB (permission keys SQL, not run) + correction to entry 31

- Change: **Correction to entry 31 (P-11): incomplete.** `permissions.key_value` is an ENUM (368 keys on staging), and P-11's three keys (`processing_change_cs_assistant`, `processing_bulk_change_owners`, `contract_details_visa_expiry_edit`) are not in it, so Settings cannot store them (strict mode rejects the save; non-strict would store an empty value). Missed earlier because the previous dumps had no `permissions` table. The new SQL appends those 3 plus the 7 S5 keys (`worker_follow_up`, `employer_follow_up`, `jo_cs_owner_follow_up`, `jo_cs_non_owner_follow_up`, `invoice_follow_up`, `insurance_follow_up`, `follow_up_transfer`). It is built from each database's CURRENT list (only missing keys appended at the end, existing keys and order untouched, so production-only keys are never dropped) and keeps the column's charset, collation and NOT NULL.
- Reason: Without it, non-admins can be granted neither the P-11 permissions nor the Follow Up keys. Tested on the staging-schema copy: 10 missing → added; 368 originals kept in order; an existing row unchanged; a new key stores; re-run does nothing; post-check 11/11 OK; rollback refuses while a key is in use.
- Status: Done (tested). Run on staging BEFORE granting these permissions.

### 66 — processing (REQ-14: bulk CS Owner / CS Assistant change with per-record review)
- File: src/services/processing.bulk.ownership.services.ts (new); src/services/index.ts; src/services/processing.services.ts (one word: `export` on isManualCsEligible); src/controllers/processing.controller.ts; src/routes/global.routes.ts; src/validations/processing.validations.ts
- REQ-ID: REQ-14 (CMATRIX §2 lines 15, 35–36; OWNERS line 25; CPERM §5 lines 45, 53); matches the prototype's Contracts Dashboard manage mode
- Change: New `POST /api/processing/bulk-ownership/preview` and `/bulk-ownership/commit`, body `{ register: cs|assistant, scope: selected|portfolio, contract_ids?, from_staff_id?, to_staff_id (null = Unassigned for assistant), reason }`. Preview evaluates every record with the commit's rules in a transaction that is always rolled back; commit re-checks every record, saves only the permitted ones (one audit entry each with reason + operation id) and returns every record's outcome (change / skip / cannot_update + reason) — no all-or-nothing. Portfolio = all Processing records of one person, resolved on the server. Permissions: processing_bulk_change_owners + register key (CS: owner / non-owner Change CS Owner per record; assistant: processing_change_cs_assistant). CS rules = the single change's (actor branch, Processing status, same owner → skip, Manual CS (P-10), new owner's branch, not the record's CS Assistant). Assistant run reuses the existing applyCsAssistantChange. CS commit sends the single change's notifications (previous + new owner) per changed record and refreshes the list cache. Limits: 100 selected, 500 portfolio (constants). The existing /bulk-change-processing-cs-assistant (all-or-nothing) and /change-processing-cs are unchanged.
- Reason: User approved after the demo. Tested on a staging-schema copy (perm-keys-sql + M-1 column applied): 13/13 cases (each outcome type with its reason; summary; preview saves nothing; commit saves only the change; one audit entry with reason + operation id; portfolio excludes non-Processing; round-robin staff refused; assistant clear + skip; 403 without the bulk key; 422 for missing reason / new owner / >100) and the notification payload (one dispatch, previous + new owner, changed record only). tsc 12 = baseline.
- Status: Done. Needs perm-keys-sql (the bulk key cannot be granted without it) and phase-m-sql M-1 (CS Assistant column). Front-end: build the review screen on these two endpoints.

### 72 — schedulers (new repo received; REQ-36 task due alerts — N1)
- File: input: src-schedulers.zip (191 files), app-schedulers.ts, package-schedulers.json, tsconfig-schedulers.json — saved as a git baseline (tsc baseline 10). Changed: src/controllers/schedule/send.task.notification.controller.ts, src/repository/task.repository.ts, src/models/task.models.ts
- REQ-ID: REQ-36 (S5 N1, HRMS_BACKEND_API_SPEC §4); answers H-4 (the scheduler service sends task due alerts, every minute)
- Change: The task due alert now (1) **claims** each task atomically (`notification_created` 0 → 1 only while the task is still outstanding) before writing its notification, so a retry or a second instance never sends a duplicate; (2) sends to the current **responsible** for a linked task, the creator for an unlinked one (was always the creator); (3) adds the N1 payload — source_type, source_id, source_ref, task_name, task_date, task_time in Hong Kong time — to json_data and to the socket event; (4) writes task history `alert_sent` (actor "System"); (5) uses the task title as the message when notes are empty (notes are optional now); (6) **fix:** when saving the notification fails, the claim is released so the alert is not lost (before, the `{ error }` result counted as success and the task was marked alerted); (7) removed `import { console } from "inspector"`, which sent this job's errors to the debugger instead of the logs. Completed / cancelled tasks are never alerted (status 1 only); reschedule and transfer rules are handled in hrms (entries 47, 52).
- Reason: Last open part of REQ-36. Tested on a staging-schema copy with h1-sql applied: 8/8 — two runs at once give exactly one alert per task; linked → responsible with source + ref + HK time; unlinked without notes → creator with title as message; completed → none; already alerted → none; alert_sent written once; socket payload carries the fields; failed save → task stays pending. tsc 10 = baseline. No DB change (uses the h1-sql columns and task_history).
- Status: Done. Not changed (finding): an alert is only sent in its exact due minute — if the scheduler is down for that minute, that task gets no alert (pre-existing behaviour).

- File: — (designs)
- REQ-ID: REQ-28 (CT-05; EXTEND §2, §5–§9; TERMINAL §6), REQ-2 (SR-03)
- Change: **S2 Extend Visa design.** New processing_extend_visa_cases (opaque case id, contract_id, state open / completed / cancelled with at most one open case per contract enforced by a unique key, Immigration status, Visa status, case-owned Visa Reference Number, E-Visa Payment Date, new Visa Expiry, cancel reason, completed / cancelled by + at, version). Start only on an Active contract without an open case (new permission "Start Extend Visa"). Visa transitions per EXTEND §7.2: Ref. No. Released needs the reference; Released only through a final confirmation checking R1–R6 and, atomically, completes + locks the case and appends an Extension issuance (S1 helper, carrying the case id) — contract status, phase and dates never change. Cancellation needs a reason and Stage View / Edit / Change Status, keeps all data, locks the case and allows a later case. The case's Immigration checklist reuses admin's checklist (REQ-21–23) scoped by the case id (extend_visa_case_id on the checklist, files, applicability and submission tables). Decisions: D-S2-1 which service owns the case API (proposed: contract, since the parent is Active; checklist parts in admin); D-S2-2 whether the visa projection (processing_visa_documents) is updated on release so existing screens show the new expiry (proposed: yes, in the same transaction); D-S2-3 a parent contract that becomes Completed / Cancelled while a case is open (EXTEND §8.2) — proposed: the case is cancelled automatically with a system reason. **S6 Versioned saves design.** A `version` column on contracts and on each processing stage table; every save sends the version it loaded; the server updates only `WHERE id = ? AND version = ?` and answers 409 "Changed by someone else — reload" otherwise; responses return the new version (already done this way for hrms tasks). Decision: D-S6-1 rollout scope (proposed: pilot on the Processing stage saves in admin + Contract Details saves in contract; version optional for one release so the current front-end keeps working, then required).
- Reason: User asked to start the two remaining not-started items (S4 on hold).
- Status: Pending approval

- File: admin: src/processing/processing.repository.ts, processing.service.ts, processing.controller.ts, src/core/i18n/resources/en.lang.json. contract: src/services/contract.visa.expiry.services.ts, src/locales/en.json. processing: src/locales/en.json (fix). Reload it and try again.") and nothing is written; responses return the new `version`; the 21 controller responses let a 409 through (other codes unchanged). **contract:** `POST /visa-expiry-update` (Active-phase visa expiry) — same optional check and returns `version`. Processing's own visa-expiry-update is left without the check (the trigger still moves the version, so admin / contract checks see its writes). **Fix found on the way:** the user's processing src/locales/en.json was invalid JSON (missing comma after STAFF_NOT_ELIGIBLE_FOR_MANUAL_CS, line 37), which can break processing's English messages or start-up; comma added.

Not run: admin integration (package.json / Prisma schema not provided). Note: the uploads folder's src.zip is now the contract repo (a later upload replaced it); the processing baseline kept from 6 Oct is unaffected.

### 90 — WS design correction (rule: biodata in_complete ⇒ status never Available / Employed; no code change yet)
- File: — (design; corrects entry 89)
- REQ-ID: WS
- Change: **User rule:** while biodata_status is in_complete, the profile status can be neither Available nor Employed. History given by the user: Employed originally came with the Active contract; it was later removed when the "current contract" concept arrived (down shift → empty current contract → biodata in_complete → Incomplete). Entry 89's E1 (Employed straight at activation) and the admin profile-edit guard break this rule. **Corrected design:** Employed is reached at the END of the profile pipeline, in the admin status engine (worker-status.engine.ts), exactly where today a verified worker with an Active contract becomes Available: Active contract and job_status ≠ "looking for new employer" (1) → **Employed**; Active contract and looking for a new employer → Available; Processing contract → Hired (unchanged); no contract → Available (unchanged). So: activation keeps the legacy down shift (Incomplete); worker fills the current contract → In Review → verified → Employed. Reverts: the down-shift status change and the activation "ensure Employed" (processing), the admin profile-edit guard. Kept: Employed tab / dropdown / actions (admin E3, E4), Inactive job (only Available), contract-end hourly job (already catches Employed workers with verified biodata). Changed: reconcile Employed without an Active contract → Available when biodata is verified, else Incomplete; backfill only Active-contract workers at Available / Hired with verified biodata and not looking for a new employer.
- Reason: User explained the biodata rule and the current-contract history and asked how to bring Employed back.
- Status: Pending approval

Mobile, processing, contract and hrms send notifications through the schedulers API, so **one central rule** in schedulers' saveNotification and bulk saveNotifications adds `job_id` to json_data: keep an existing job_id; else a top-level job_id; else rel_id for job_matched / job_start_date_update; else interview_bookings.job_id for interview types (one lookup per batch); otherwise unchanged — never guessed. admin's interview module inserts notifications directly, so the same booking lookup is added there. **Bug found and fixed:** `job_start_date_update` is not a value of staging's notifications.type ENUM, so that schedulers notification could never be saved — jo-sql appends it (existing values untouched; re-run safe).
- Reason: User asked to continue ("next steps karo"); deploys everything together. Tests: N-JO 8/8 against a test DB (bulk save; job_matched → rel_id; interview → booking's job (bulk and single, null json_data); top-level job_id copied; existing job_id kept; non-Job-Offer notification untouched; unknown booking → nothing added, still saved); jo-sql with the ENUM step — re-run safe, post-check 4/4 OK, job_start_date_update now saves, existing values unaffected. Schedulers tsc 0 new; admin syntax OK; both patches apply cleanly.
- Status: Done — **JO Phase 1 complete** (P1-a entry 96, P1-b entry 97, N-JO). Deploy order: jo-sql → admin (admin-jo-p1a, admin-jo-p1b, admin-jo-njo) → schedulers (schedulers-jo-njo).

### 123 — contract: "Host 'staging' is not configured" (diagnosis, no code change)
- File: input: src-contract.zip, app.ts, 5 screenshots (POST /api/contract/global/list → 400 {"message":"Host 'staging' is not configured"}; frontend shows "Something went wrong")
- REQ-ID: — (environment / local setup)
- Change: Cause: the user's app.ts hardcodes `const originHeader = "staging"` and passes it to getHostType(), which only accepts values listed in STAGING_HOSTS / PRODUCTION_HOSTS — those hold URLs (e.g. http://localhost:3000), so the literal "staging" is never found and every contract request fails with 400 (the frontend Contracts page then errors). Fixes offered: A) add `staging` to STAGING_HOSTS in the contract .env; B) use the frontend's `Hosttype` header (already sent) in app.ts, falling back to Origin / Referer; C) restore the commented Origin / Referer line and keep http://localhost:3000 in STAGING_HOSTS. Also noted: debug console.log lines in getHostType ("his"); other services may have the same hardcode.
- Reason: User asked what the problem is.
- Status: Waiting for the user to apply one fix

### 143 — admin: Nest build error TS1206 "Decorators are not valid here" (my mistake) — fixed
- File: input: src-admin.zip (user's latest admin: contains the JO work, the activity filters, no withdrawn WS guard) → new admin baseline (admin4 / admin3). Output: src/interview/interview.repository.ts, src/jobs/jobs.repository.ts — admin-decorator-fix.patch, admin-decorator-fix-files.zip, admin-updated.zip (rebuilt on this src). DEPLOY_CHECKLIST.md §2 updated.
- REQ-ID: JO D-05 (entry 104) and JO D-18 (entry 96) side effect
- Change: In entries 96 / 104 I inserted a top-level helper (`nestInterviewOwners`) and a constant (`JOB_LIST_JOINS`) immediately before `export class …` — but NestJS classes carry `@Injectable()` on the line above, so the insert landed BETWEEN the decorator and the class and the decorator ended up on a const → TS1206, the server could not start. The decorator lines are moved back directly above the classes. A scan of the whole admin src finds no other decorator followed by a non-class line.
- Reason: User's screenshot of the failing watch build. Checks: both files transpile with decorators enabled; scan 0 problems. **Note for future edits:** when inserting module-level code before a Nest class, insert ABOVE its decorators (`@Injectable()`, `@Controller()`), never between decorator and class.
- Status: Done — replace the 2 files (or admin-updated.zip) and restart

### 158 — admin: login "Timed out fetching a new connection from the connection pool" (diagnosis, no code change)
- The uploaded admin `src.zip` is byte-identical to admin-updated.zip (entry 153-era build) — nothing on the login path was changed by us; `AuthService.login → checkUserEmail` is a plain `SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ? LIMIT 1`.
- Prisma P2024 = no MySQL connection could be obtained within `DB_POOL_TIMEOUT` (10 s, `DB_CONNECTION_LIMIT` 10). The DB the request uses is chosen per request by `HostTypeMiddleware` from the frontend's `hosttype` header (`REACT_APP_HOST_TYPE`) → `DB_*_LOCAL / _STAGING / _PRODUCTION` block, falling back to `DATABASE_URL` / `DB_*` only when that block is incomplete. The boot-time `$connect()` ping uses `DB_DEFAULT_HOST_TYPE` instead, so "boots fine but every request times out" = the request block points at a different (unreachable / slow / full) MySQL than the boot block.
- Checks given to the user: which block the header selects, reachability of that host, `SHOW STATUS LIKE 'Threads_connected'` vs `max_connections`, metadata locks from DBeaver DDL (`Waiting for table metadata lock`), then `DB_POOL_TIMEOUT` / `DB_CONNECTION_LIMIT` as a tuning knob only.
- Status: Info
- **Confirmed from the user's admin `.env` (entry 158):** `DB_DEFAULT_HOST_TYPE=staging` and the frontend sends `hosttype: staging`, and the STAGING block is complete — `DB_HOST_STAGING=13.212.37.215` (remote AWS MySQL, port 3306 by default). So every request, and the boot ping, go to the remote server, never to the local `127.0.0.1:3307/maidmatch_staging_beta_v2` where the schema changes were applied; the laptop cannot open that connection (security group) → pool timeout. Fix given: point the STAGING block at the local DB (`DB_HOST_STAGING=127.0.0.1`, `DB_PORT_STAGING=3307`, `DB_DATABASE_STAGING=maidmatch_staging_beta_v2`, `DB_USERNAME_STAGING=root`, empty password), and correct `DATABASE_URL` / the LOCAL block, which still name the OLD database `maidmatch_staging_beta` (and port 3306).

### 162 — local processing API: every request answers `400 {"error":"Host 'staging' is not configured"}` (diagnosis; no repo code change)
- Symptom (user screenshot): `POST http://127.0.0.1:8001/api/processing/global/bulk-ownership/preview` → 400 Bad Request, Content-Length 44; the dialog showed "Something went wrong".
- Cause (read from the linked computer, `D:\Projects\Overseas\maidmatch-processing-api`): the bulk-ownership service, validation, routes, controller and error handler there are byte-identical to ours. But two local edits (both dated 2026-10-08 16:26 IST) hardcode the host type — `app.ts` onRequest hook `const origin = "staging"` (the header line is commented out) and `src/middlewares/verify.token.middlware.ts:288` `getHostType("staging")`. `getHostType()` matches the value against the hostnames in `STAGING_HOSTS` (`https://staging.maidmatch.ai/,http://localhost:3000/,localhost:8001` → `staging.maidmatch.ai`, `localhost`); the literal `staging` is not one of them, so it throws `Host 'staging' is not configured` and the onRequest hook answers 400 `{"error":"Host 'staging' is not configured"}` — exactly 44 bytes. Every route of the local processing API (not only bulk-ownership; only the lokalise/translate paths are exempt) fails the same way, so Contract Details' Processing calls would too.
- Fix (user applies; `.env` cannot be written through the link): either append `,staging` to `STAGING_HOSTS` in the processing `.env` and restart — or revert the two hardcodes to `req.headers.origin || req.headers.referer` (localhost:3000 is already an allowed staging host). The hardcodes are in tracked files — do not commit / deploy them.
- Also seen (cleanup, untouched): the processing repo contains stray frontend files `src/Views/Pages/Contract/details/ContractDetails.js` and `src/Views/Pages/ProcessingPage/processing-detail/ProcessingDetail.js` (dated 2026-10-08).
- Frontend (committed to the project + zips): `BulkOwnershipModal.js` `apiError()` now also shows a server `{ error: "…" }` body and the HTTP status instead of the bare "Something went wrong", so this kind of failure is readable in the dialog.
- Status: Info — frontend tweak done; the local backend config is the user's to change.

### 170 — HRMS API (staging server): calendar list "Failed to fetch" = Redis hanging; fail-fast guards (hrms-redis-guard.patch)
- Files (hrms-redis-guard.patch, appended to hrms.patch; hrms-updated.zip / hrms-changed-files.zip): `src/config/redis.connect.config.ts`, `src/services/redis.services.ts`, `src/utils/token.cache.utils.ts`. Nothing in the frontend.
- REQ-ID: user — `https://hrmsapi-v2.maidmatch.ai/api/hrms/calendar/list?from=2026-10-05&to=2026-10-11&search_user_id=1` fails on the server, works locally, same staging DB.
- Diagnosis (measured from her Chrome with her app token, origin http://localhost:3000): every endpoint that reads Redis dies after ~10.2 s with no response — calendar/list (range, month and no-user variants), profile/details, leave-apply/type-list — while every endpoint that never touches Redis answers in ~180 ms (calendar/users-list, task/list, task/templates). Server up (nginx 1.28 + the current build: S11 translate guard, "Api key is required" / "Apii key is invalid"), host gate passes for localhost, token valid. So the staging HRMS process cannot reach its Redis (ElastiCache: REDIS_HOST / REDIS_PORT / REDIS_TLS (defaults to true) / REDIS_PASSWORD in the server .env, or a security group); node-redis keeps reconnecting, `isOpen` stays true, and a GET issued meanwhile sits in the offline queue for ever, so `getCache` never returns, the request never answers and the proxy cuts it at its read timeout (≈10 s) — "Failed to fetch" in the browser because the error page carries no CORS headers. Locally Redis is reachable (or IS_REDIS_ENABLED=false), so the same code works. Log signature on the server: repeated "❌ Redis error: …" after start, no "✅ Redis connected".
- Server fix (hers): either fix the REDIS_* values (`redis-cli -h <host> -p <port> --tls ping` from the server must answer PONG; REDIS_TLS=false if the cluster has no in-transit encryption) or set `IS_REDIS_ENABLED=false` and restart — the service runs without cache.
- Code hardening so a dead Redis can never hang requests again: `disableOfflineQueue: true` on the client (a command issued while the socket is down/reconnecting rejects at once); the cache helpers (get/set/del/update/deletePattern/clearAll) and the token-verification cache skip Redis unless `isReady` (`isOpen` is true while merely reconnecting) — a not-ready Redis behaves exactly like a disabled one (cache miss → DB).
- Checks: TypeScript transpile of the three files clean; no behaviour change when Redis is healthy (helpers unchanged after the guard).
- Status: Done — 2026-10-09 20:59 IST user confirmed: `REDIS_TLS=true` set on the HRMS staging server and hrms-redis-guard.patch deployed.

### 171 — Contract Details header: Actions menu, Change Status dialogs and Manage owners drawer as the prototype (same APIs)

Complete Contract: intro naming the Expected Contract End (reached → Finished allowed; before it → only an early-ending reason, Finished disabled with "Finished — from <date>"), Reason, Start Date (read-only), Contract End (defaults to the expected end for Finished, else today; after Contract Start · not in the future), the "Expected Contract End <date> — Contract Start + 2 years." note, Remarks (required for an early ending). Completed → Active and Cancelled → Processing: the 440px confirmations with the prototype's intro copy; the Visa-Expiry block variant ("Cannot return to Active" + the Contract Start / Visa Expiry note + Close). Manage owners: the fixed right drawer (540px + border, 16/20 head "Manage owners" over "code · helper", 55px rows: 30px tinted avatar · name 12.5/500 · role 11px · 26px "Change"), rows Processing CS Owner · CS Assistant · Prepared owner · Ready owner (the last two "—", no action — no register behind them yet). Change <register>: intro "helper · code · Group g · branch. Current <register>: <name>.", the `.ctx-picks` radio list (36px rows, the current holder disabled "current", the record's CS Assistant tagged "CS Assistant" in the owner list, "Unassigned — clears the register" for the assistant), the owner/assistant collision note with "Also set CS Assistant to Unassigned on this record", the reason field (assistant), the register note, Cancel + "Change <register>" / "Set CS Assistant to Unassigned" (disabled until a valid pick). Owners pill: 8px gap, 0 10 padding, #e3e7ed 7px, 22px avatars with the 2px white ring overlapping 7px, "Owners" 12.5/550, mono count.
- Functionality (the APIs the Processing screen already uses, nothing new on the server): every status change = `POST /processing/global/status-update` (updateProcessingGlobalSatusList) with the same fields as before — Cancelled: `cancellation_reason` (gbo | ebo | medically_unfit | other) · `cancellation_date` · `remarks`; Completed: `completion_reason` (the worker contract-status id from `fetchContractStatus({type:'worker'})`; Finished = id 1) · `expiry_date` (the Contract End) · `remarks`; Active: `expiry_date` (the API requires it — the recorded Contract End is sent, and only when that date has already passed the dialog asks for a new one, today or later, as the server demands); Processing: status only. The Contract Start < Visa Expiry invariant is checked before Completed and Completed → Active with `hasContractStartVisaConflict` as before (the server re-checks). Owner changes: Processing CS Owner = `POST /processing/global/change-processing-cs` (`staff_id`, `send_email` — the "Send notification email" tick of the old popup is kept as a checkbox — and `cs_assistant_action: "remove"` when the recipient is the record's CS Assistant and the box is ticked; without the Change CS Assistant permission the collision shows the old "You need the Change Processing CS Assistant permission" message and stays blocked); CS Assistant = `POST /processing/global/change-processing-cs-assistant` (`staff_id`, null for Unassigned, optional `reason`); recipients from `POST /settings/employer/search-other-cs-staff` (type contract, rel_id, employer_id) as both old popups used. Success → the same toasts (showSuccessOrError / ShowToast), the record and the status list refresh; a server refusal stays in the dialog as the red note. Escape / backdrop / × close; focus is trapped (useDialogFocus); the dialogs and the drawer render through a portal so the header's stacking does not clip them. The status list still comes from `/processing/global/status-list` (its `enable` flags decide the targets; the current status is the greyed row).
- Not done / limits: the prototype's Active → Processing "Revert" flow (reason, stages to reopen, post-hire block, review step) has no backend — that target opens a plain confirmation ("The contract returns to the Processing dashboard. Contract Start, stage data, uploaded files and owners are kept.") and sends the status change the API accepts; the Start Date in Complete Contract is read-only for everyone (the status API takes no Start correction; the prototype lets Processing > Edit holders correct it); Prepared / Ready owners have no register; the prototype's "Inactive" badge needs staff status the row does not carry; the reason field of the CS Owner change is omitted because that API has no reason (the assistant one has). Edit keeps opening the employer profile editor (the prototype's Edit is only a toast stub).
- Checks: ESLint clean (only the pre-existing `process` warning in ContractDetails.js); rendered in headless Chromium with the real dialogs / drawer (stubbed services) against the prototype captures — cancel 560×367.8 / 419.1 (GBO + errors), complete 496.1, confirmations 440×159.5, blocked 260.8, reassign 528.6 (assistant) and the collision note 107.1: every row / field / input / note / button box equal to the prototype's, picks 36px with the 15/21px dots; flows exercised: cancel (EBO + remarks → one status-update call), complete (Terminated, remarks required → call), Cancelled → Processing, Completed → Active (expired end → new date), visa-blocked, Manage owners → Change CS Owner (pick → change-processing-cs call, drawer stays open), Change CS Assistant (Unassigned pick), the collision checkbox; the CRA dev server recompiled without an error overlay — the ContractDetails chunk carries the new menu, the ContractStatusDialogs / ManageOwnersDrawer chunks exist and main.js has the new en.json keys. ### 176 — Contract Details: Requested Start Date saved but still "—" — the local contract API was behind; a partial details-update must not clear the Expected Start Date (written directly into the project)
- Files: `D:\Projects\Overseas\maidmatch-contract\src\services\contract.dashboard.services.ts` (the entry 153/156 version of dashboard-list, until now only delivered as contract-updated.zip / contract-dashboard-fix.patch — the folder was linked for this), `D:\Projects\Overseas\maidmatch-processing-api\src\services\processing.services.ts` (`processingDetailsUpdate`), `D:\Projects\Overseas\maidmatch-admin-reactjs\src\Views\Pages\Contract\details\ContractDetails.js`.
- REQ-ID: user, 2026-10-12 11:15 IST, screenshot of /contracts/details/6 — "react frontend mai save to ho raha hai but show nehi kar raha hai; direct folder mai jaker update karo".
- Cause: the page shows `row.requested_start_date` from `POST /contract/global/dashboard-list`; the copy of that service in the local `maidmatch-contract` repo was the pre-entry-153 version — no `requested_start_date` / `expected_start_date`, no `cancellation_date` / `cancellation_reason` / `completion_reason`, no `paos`, no `worker_user_id`, and the old sort keys — so the save (processing API) succeeded but the re-fetched row never carried the date (the same reason Chat History showed 0 / "No worker account linked" locally). Second problem found on the way: `/processing/global/details-update` wrote `expected_start_date` and `requested_start_date` unconditionally (null when absent) — the Contract Details card sends only the requested date, so every save there was wiping the Expected Start Date.
- Fix: the current dashboard-list service written into the contract repo (adds the fields above; PAOS from `processing_paos_cases`, which exists on staging — entry 144); the processing API now writes only the date keys the request carries (the Processing form still sends both, unchanged behaviour); the page also sends the recorded `expected_start_date` back unchanged with a Requested Start Date save, so the data is safe even against an un-restarted processing API.
- Checks: her copies of both backend files were byte-identical (line endings aside) to our bases before the write; the dashboard diff is self-contained (no new imports); ESLint clean on ContractDetails.js (the known `process` warning only).
- Status: Done — restart the contract API and the processing API locally (and redeploy both on the server if the staging ones are behind as well); the frontend recompiles on its own.

### 177 — Contract Details · Context → Remarks: the prototype rail card + the delivered Remarks drawer (composer, remark cards, author-window edit / delete, delete confirmation) — written directly into the project
- Files: `src/Views/Pages/Contract/details/ContractRemarksDrawer.js` (new), `src/Views/Pages/Contract/details/ContextPanelSections.js`, `src/Views/Pages/Contract/details/contract-actions.css`, `src/Views/Pages/Contract/details/ContractDetails.css`, `src/components/mc-dialog/McDialog.js` + `mc-dialog.css` (new shared `AlertDialog`), `public/locales/en/translation.json` (+15 keys), `src/i18n/fallback/en.json` (+17 keys).

- Before: the rail card expanded in place and showed the legacy `RemarksCollapseView` inside it (bootstrap card header, "Add Remark" link, remark blocks with trash / pencil icons, 500-char textarea with Cancel / Save links, "Confirm delete" popup); edit / delete icons were offered on every own remark and on others' remarks with the `update_else` permission, which the processing API refuses.
- Design (prototype): `Contract_Details.html` railContextHTML — the Remarks card is `rowHead('Remarks', n)` (13px/600 title · mono 11.5px count · 12px chevron) with "No remarks yet" under it and opens "this panel" (`railCard`); the delivered panel is the shared `.ctx-drawer` Remarks panel of `Worker_Details.html` (remarksPanelHTML / remarkItemHTML / `.ctx-confirm`), whose CSS the Contract Details prototype already carries (`.remark-composer`, `.remark-item`, `.ctx-state`, `.ctx-confirm`). Captured with `tools/proto-remarks.mjs` → `/home/claude/proto-remarks/` (rail card, drawer list / typed / editing / empty, delete confirm, Worker Details rail preview).
- Now: the card no longer expands — the whole card is the button (hover #f4f6f8, Enter / Space) and opens the Remarks drawer; its second line is "No remarks yet" or the latest remark (two lines, clamped — Worker Details ctx-preview). The card exists only with the processing remarks permission (prototype `pcan('remarks')`; the list API is gated on the same keys), and the count / preview appear once this record's list has loaded (the slice starts with `[]`, so no stale count from the previous record).
- Drawer (`ContractRemarksDrawer`): the `.mcca-drawer` frame (540px + 1px border, head "Remarks" + "worker · code" mono, ×), body gap 16px: composer (label "Add a Contract remark", 84px textarea, placeholder "Add operational context…", mono `n/500` counter — 500 is the API's `remarks: max:500`, not the Worker panel's 1000 — primary "Add Remark" disabled while empty) and the list: `article.remark-item` (14px padding) with 26px initials avatar, name 12.5/550 + "(staff)" (the list joins `staffs`), mono `DD MMM YYYY · HH:mm` time, the text 12.5/1.5 #3d4552 (`pre-wrap`, stored content shown as is), then "Authored by you · Edit · Delete" (10.5px / 11.5px link buttons, Delete #b42318) or the lock line. Edit loads the text into the composer ("Save Remark" + a Cancel; the card being edited is tinted), Delete opens the prototype confirmation (`AlertDialog`: "Delete this remark?", the soft-delete copy, Cancel / "Delete Remark" danger, 460px, Escape / Cancel only — the backdrop does not close it, as in the prototype). Loading / error (with Retry) / empty states are the prototype `.ctx-state` blocks (308px as rendered there).
- Rules: the processing API lets only the author change or delete a remark and only within 24 hours of posting (`saveRemarks` / `deleteRemarks`: `remarks_by == admin` and `hours <= 24`; `update_else` permission keys are not honoured there), so Edit / Delete appear on exactly those remarks; an own remark past the window gets "Editing closed — a remark can be changed or deleted within 24 hours of posting.", others' remarks "Only this remark’s author can edit or delete it." (prototype line). The clock is the browser's against the API's `created_at` (already in the admin's timezone); the API stays the authority and its refusal shows as the toast.
- Same API as before: `fetchProcessingRemarks` → `POST /get-processing-remarks-list { contract_id }`; `addProcessingRemark` / `updateProcessingRemark` → `POST /save-processing-remarks { contract_id, remarks[, remark_id] }`; `deleteProcessingRemark` → `POST /delete-processing-remarks { remark_id }`. The list is re-read after every change (the update echo carries no `created_at` / author name, which the old in-place reducer replace left blank), and the rail count / preview follow.
- `AlertDialog` (McDialog.js / mc-dialog.css `.mcca-alert`) is the prototype `.ctx-confirm` for the new UI — role alertdialog, focus trap, Escape — for any short destructive confirmation; `.mcca-drawer .ctx-btn` (30px primary / secondary) is now a drawer-level rule (the owners rows keep their 26px variant).
- Translation: new keys with English fallbacks through `tr()` (so nothing renders raw): `key_add_a_contract_remark`, `key_remark_save` ("Save Remark" — the existing `key_save_remark` is "Save remark"), `key_follow_up_title` ("Follow Up" — the existing `key_follow_up` is "Follow up"), `key_no_remarks_yet`, `key_add_first_contract_remark`, `key_remark_author_only`, `key_remark_window_closed`, `key_staff_lower`, `key_loading_remarks`, `key_retrieving_record_data`, `key_delete_this_remark`, `key_delete_remark_copy`, `key_delete_remark`, `key_remark_updated`, `key_remark_deleted` (+ `key_source_data_not_changed`, `key_remark_added`, already in translation.json). Her translation.json (5427 keys, +525 calendar / worker keys since entry 175) was the base. To be put on the Google Sheet as usual.

### 180 — Settings API · Postman "Port should be >= 0 and < 65536 ('5003api')" on branch/mapping-list (diagnosis; no code change)
- REQ-ID: user, 2026-10-12 13:16 IST, Postman screenshot — `GET {{settings_backend}}api/settings/branch/mapping-list?module_key=worker` → "Could not send request · Port should be >= 0 and < 65536. Received type string ('5003api')".
- Cause: a URL-building slip, not the API. The environment variable `settings_backend` ends without a slash (`http://localhost:5003`, like the frontend's `REACT_APP_SETTINGS_API_URL`, which the app completes with `+ '/api'`), and the request path starts with `api/…` without one, so the request is sent to `http://localhost:5003api/settings/branch/mapping-list` — Node reads the port as `5003api` and refuses before anything leaves Postman.
- Fix (Postman only): `{{settings_backend}}/api/settings/branch/mapping-list` (or give the variable a trailing slash). Checked in `maidmatch-settings-api`: `server.js` registers `branchRouter` at `/api/settings/branch`, `routes/branch.route.js` has `GET /mapping-list` (token required, no permission middleware), and `services/branch.services.js` `branchMappingList` reads only `module_key`, `branch_country_code`, `agency_type` from the query — `search_text` / `status` are ignored there (they belong to `/list`); non-admins need `module_key` (worker → `worker_checkin_branch` permission values), admins get every active branch; the port comes from `NODE_PORT` in `.env` (default 5001).
- Status: Done — nothing to write in the repos.

Text that matches none of the forms returns no rows (head_line is no longer searched). `device_type` stays accepted beside `current_engagement`.
- Reason: User go-ahead "A start karo" on the 12 Oct list. Tests: search clause 20 inputs (phone forms, email, HKID forms, JO ref forms, names, CJK, mixed, symbols) — expected SQL/args; ORDER BY captured through a fake DB for status asc/desc and last_activity; TypeScript check clean on the three files.

- File: `src/jobs/jobs.controller.ts` (POST change-job-owner, GET/POST get-eligible-cs-staff, ownerKey by Job Offer), `src/jobs/jobs.service.ts` (changeJobOwner, eligibleCsStaff), `src/jobs/jobs.repository.ts` (jobOwnerRow, eligibleCsStaff, staffEligibilityRow), `src/audit/job-log-writer.service.ts` (change_job_owner), `src/auth/permission.service.ts` (jo_cs_owner_change_owner / jo_cs_non_owner_change_owner as y/n keys), `src/core/i18n/resources/{en,in,zh_CN,zh_HK}.lang.json` (10 keys). SQL: `jo3-sql.zip` (01 pre-check · 02 migration · 03 post-check · 04 rollback).

- Change: **`POST /change-job-owner { job_id, cs_owner_staff_id, reason? }`** — permission `jo_cs_owner_change_owner` when the caller owns this Job Offer, else `jo_cs_non_owner_change_owner` (both keys existed in the settings list but the permission checker did not know them — added to its y/n set); admin bypasses. Sets `jobs.cs_owner_staff_id` (the employer's CS and the employer's other Job Offers are untouched), stores the reason in `jobs.cs_change_reason` / `cs_change_reason_updated_at`, writes a `job_logs` row "JO000340 CS Owner changed from Linda Wong to Ian Chan" (log_type change_job_owner), and notifies through the schedulers API: the new owner with the existing type `assign_employer_cs` ("Job Offer __JOB_REFERENCE_ID__ has been transferred to you from __OLD_CS__."), the previous owner with the new type `jo_owner_transferred` ("… transferred from you to __NEW_CS__."), both with json_data { job_id, employer_id, previous_cs_owner_id, cs_owner_id, names, reason, changed_by } — two calls, so a rejected type never loses the other. 422 when the staff does not exist / is not a CS / is inactive / is not internal (or is an admin role) / already owns it; validation errors 422; no permission 403. Response: { job_id, reference_id, cs_owner_id, cs_owner_name, cs_owner_user_id, previous_cs_owner_id, previous_cs_owner_name, reason }. **`GET|POST /get-eligible-cs-staff ?job_id=&branch_id=&language_id=&search=`** — internal, active, is_cs staff (not admin roles); `branch_id` (one or CSV) filters `cs_branches`, `language_id` filters `cs_employer_language`, `search` the name; with `job_id` the Job Offer's employer branch is the default branch filter and the current owner is marked `is_current_owner`. **Owner resolution fixed**: `ownerKey()` (get-job-detail, save-job-offer edit, change-job-offer-status) now decides owner / non-owner by the Job Offer's owner (`COALESCE(jobs.cs_owner_staff_id, employers.cs_owner_id)`) instead of the employer's CS, so a transferred Job Offer is governed by its new owner. The notification copy lives in the lang files (all four locales carry the English text) — edit there if wording should change. `notifications.type` is an ENUM on staging: `jo3-sql.zip` appends `jo_owner_transferred` (re-run safe; without it only the previous owner's notification is dropped by the schedulers).
- Reason: 12 Oct list (item 3; the notification copy is a default the user may change). Tests: 16/16 with a fake DB (unknown job; inactive / not CS / partner / missing staff / same owner → 422 and no writes; success → UPDATE jobs with owner + reason, job_logs message, two notifications with the right users / types / payload; eligible list by job → branch from the employer, current owner marked; branch CSV + language + search filters). TypeScript check clean on all files; the patch applies cleanly to the repo copies.

- File: SQL `jo4-sql.zip` (01 pre-check · 02 migration · 03 post-check · 04 rollback — also carries entries 198–200's columns). admin: `src/jobs/jobs.constants.ts` (CLOSED_REASON, notDeletedJobSql), `src/jobs/jobs.repository.ts` (list / owner filters, closed_reason in core, list and detail selects), `src/jobs/jobs.service.ts` (changeStatus), `src/employer/employer.repository.ts` (employer job-offer list), `src/reference/reference.service.ts` + `reference.repository.ts` (/get-job-statuses without 4; no transitions for a deleted Job Offer; jobClosedReason). scheduler: `src/models/job.model.ts` (closed_reason), `src/controllers/schedule/update.job.status.expired.controller.ts` (closed_reason = expired), `src/repository/job.repository.ts` (after-7/14/30/60-days notifications only for closed_reason = expired). mobile: `src/employer/employer.constants.helper.ts` (JobClosedReason, notDeletedJobSql), `src/job/job.service.ts` (delete → Closed / deleted; close → manual), `src/job/job-serializer.helper.ts` + `src/job/job-db.helper.ts` + `src/employer/employer.service.ts` (deleted Job Offers excluded as status 9 was).

Lists: get-job-list, status_counts, get-employer-job-offer-list and the owner lookup exclude `status = 6 AND closed_reason = 'deleted'` exactly as they excluded 9, and rows / the detail carry `closed_reason`. `/get-job-statuses` (no job_id) now offers Draft 7 · In Review 2 · Open 1 · On Hold 3 · Closed 6 (4 is a system state; 5 / 8 / 9 never offered). **scheduler:** the expiry cron already set Closed (6) — it now also writes closed_reason = expired, and the 7 / 14 / 30 / 60-days-after-expiry employer notifications target only those (a manual close no longer gets "your job expired" messages). **mobile:** DELETE_JOB_OFFER stores Closed / deleted (+ deleted_by/at, closing_date_time), CLOSE_JOB_OFFER stores closed_reason manual; the employer home counts and job lists exclude deleted Job Offers as before. Frontend: no change needed (Closed card / status pill unchanged; `closed_reason` available on rows for a later "Closed · expired" label).
- Reason: Approved. Checks: TypeScript check clean on every admin / mobile / scheduler file (pre-existing unresolved-type notes aside); the SQL uses the same information_schema-guarded pattern as jo-sql (02) with pre- and post-checks. ### 205 — scheduler · interview auto-complete 30 minutes after the scheduled time, every run (JO 12 Oct #8, U-0227 option A)
- File: `maidmatch-scheduler/src/repository/interview.bookings.repository.ts` (getInterviewsToBeAutoCompleteAfter30Min) — written into the repo; `scheduler-deploy/` copy; `schedulers-updated.zip` refreshed.
- REQ-ID: 12 Oct item 8 — user chose **A** (auto-complete only; the forced report popup stays off; the socket event `interviews_to_complete` stays disabled)
- Change: the 30-minute auto-complete job (cron at :00 and :30) matched only interviews whose scheduled time + 30 minutes fell exactly on the current minute (`DATE_FORMAT(interview_time + 30 MINUTE, '%H:%i') = DATE_FORMAT(UTC_TIMESTAMP(), '%H:%i')`), so an interview at 10:15 (due 10:45) was never caught and stayed Upcoming until the end-of-day job. It now selects every not-rescheduled Upcoming interview of today or yesterday whose `TIMESTAMP(interview_date, interview_time) + 30 MINUTE <= UTC_TIMESTAMP()`, so each run completes everything due since the previous run (same completion path as before: Completed status, `completed_by_cron`, `interview_report_marked_by_system`, job stage, log `interview_request_auto_completed_after_30_min`, notifications). `markTodaysInterviewsAutoComplete` (end of day) is unchanged as the safety net. Not changed: `getInterviewsToBeCompleted` (socket emit) stays commented out in the cron list; the frontend popup stays commented out.
- Reason: User decision "A". Check: TypeScript check clean; the repo copy equalled the delivered version before the edit; the Sequelize `fn` / `literal` pattern is the one the original condition used.
- Status: Done — restart the scheduler. Note: entry 204 was missing again from the repo's CHANGE_HISTORY.md (an editor re-saved an older buffer) and is re-added with this entry.

### 212 — scheduler · 2-year auto-complete selects ACTIVE contracts (was Processing) + Visa Expiry guard (audit D1)
- File: `maidmatch-scheduler/src/repository/contract.repository.ts` (getContractListToBeCompleted) — written into the repo.
- REQ-ID: PROCESS §2.4.3 (2-year rule), TERMINAL §4 (Start < Visa Expiry, no override, blank never blocks); audit 13 Oct D1.
- Change: the midnight job `updateContractStatusCompleted` selected `status = contractInvoicesStatus.processing` (1) with `expiry_date < CURDATE()`, so overdue Processing contracts were completed with "> 2 Years" and Active contracts never were. Now: `status = active` (2), `expiry_date <= CURDATE()` (not null), and a NOT EXISTS guard that leaves a contract Active when its recorded Visa Expiry (first visa row of the contract, as the app reads it) is not after the Contract Start. Completion path unchanged (Completed, completion_reason "> 2 Years", updated_by_cron, status log).
- Reason: audit. Check: scheduler tsc — no new errors (baseline noise only).
- Status: Done — restart the scheduler.

### 213 — admin API · E-Visa save no longer derives / overwrites Visa Expiry (audit D2)
- File: `maidmatch-admin-api-nodejs/src/processing/processing.service.ts` (updateVisaEvisa) — written into the repo.
- REQ-ID: TERMINAL §5 "Visa Expiry is never derived — recorded", CT-05, EXTEND §3; audit D2.
- Change: every E-Visa autosave wrote `visa_expiry_date = e_visa_payment_date + 6 months (G1/2) / + 2 years (G3/4)` and `null` when the payment date was cleared — overwriting the recorded value with no history row or issuance correction. The save now leaves `visa_expiry_date` untouched; it is recorded / corrected only through the processing API's Record / Update Visa Expiry (reason + `processing_visa_expiry_history` + issuance correction). Reference / payment-date saves unchanged. `addToDate` kept (used elsewhere).
- Reason: audit. Check: admin tsc clean (0 errors). The E-Visa tab already shows the recorded value through `VisaExpiryEditor`.
- Status: Done — restart the admin API.

### 214 — admin API + frontend · Immigration: sign-off rules in the real save path, applicability in the readiness gate, Submitted lock (audit D4–D6)
- File: `maidmatch-admin-api-nodejs/src/processing/immigration-checklist.ts` (new `immigrationGate`, `sectionsInFields`, `sectionSignOffsToClear`, `NEVER_BLOCKING_REQUIREMENTS`), `processing.service.ts` (updateStatus, statusList, checkContractTabStatus, updateFullDetails, setApplicability, uploadFiles, deleteFiles, associateFile, new `assertImmigrationNotSubmitted`), `processing.controller.ts` (update-immigration-applicability passes the field list), `src/core/i18n/resources/en.lang.json` (+`IMMIGRATION_SUBMITTED_READ_ONLY`, `IMMIGRATION_APPLICABILITY_REQUIRED`); frontend `src/Views/Pages/ProcessingPage/processing-detail/immigration-tab/ProcessingImmigrationTab.js`.
- REQ-ID: EXTEND §5.2 / §5.3 / §5.4 / §5.5 / §5.8 / §5.9 / §5.12, CT-03; audit D4, D5, D6.
- Change: (1) **Ready requires Prepared / clearing Prepared clears Ready** now runs in `updateFullDetails` — the save the checklist screens actually use (the rule lived only in `updateDocuments`, which refuses Immigration); the Immigration row is read group-aware so stored Prepared marks are seen. (2) **One readiness gate** (`immigrationGate`) for the status change, the status-list `enable` flag and the checklist-save recompute: every conditional section on the checklist needs a recorded assessment (none / Needs review blocks with `IMMIGRATION_APPLICABILITY_REQUIRED` — unknown is not No), rows of an `applies` section count, `does_not_apply` rows are excluded; ID 522, the physical original's Uploaded, the Financial Support marriage / birth certificates (CT-03) and Optional never block. The recompute used to read the base 5-column row, so group-specific fields always looked empty. (3) **Changing an assessment** resets that section's Prepared / Ready marks, re-evaluates the whole gate and drops Ready to Submit to Not Ready when it fails — one Activity entry carries the assessment change, the cleared marks and the status reset. (4) **Submitted is read-only**: checklist saves, assessment changes, requirement-linked uploads, linked-file deletes and re-links answer `IMMIGRATION_SUBMITTED_READ_ONLY` while Immigration is Submitted (unlinked uploads stay allowed; Returned reopens). (5) `updateFullDetails` honours an optional `version` (409) and returns `version` + the recomputed `status`. Frontend: un-ticking Prepared un-ticks Ready in the same change (all 8 handlers); every row control is disabled while Immigration is Submitted.
- Reason: audit. Check: admin tsc 0 errors; en.lang.json parses; frontend ESLint clean; full esbuild bundle of src/index.js resolves. zh_HK / zh_CN / in fall back to English for the two new keys (localization team).
- Status: Done — restart the admin API, frontend rebuild.

- REQ-ID: PROCESS §2.4.4 ("All field changes (Contract End, Reason) are reversed"), §2.5.4 ("Reason, Cancelled Date reversed"), TERMINAL §2; audit D13 + the activation label gap.

The Groups 1–2 activation Activity event now carries `trigger: "end_of_deployment"` (was end_of_processing).
- Reason: audit. Check: processing tsc — no new errors (baseline noise only).

### 218 — final re-check of entries 212–217 (two independent code reviews + unit runs); eight follow-up fixes
- File: admin `src/processing/processing.repository.ts` (IMM_G34_LOCAL_TERMINATED_TR, IMM_G34_LOCAL_RENEWAL, the Group 1/2 branch), `processing.service.ts` (unused import); contract `src/services/invoices.services.ts` + `v1/invoices.services.ts` (contract-category-list returns `processing_group`; `/invoices/list` accepts `contract_id` and returns it); frontend `ContractsDashboard.js`, `details/ContractDetails.js`, `contract-tab/ProcessingContractTab.js`.
- REQ-ID: user 13 Oct 11:19 IST — "mere man ki shanti ke liye phir se dekh lo, final".
- Change: (1) **Immigration picked-column lists** did not cover every field of `processing-fields.ts`: Group 3/4 Local Terminated TR (cat 4) lacked the `contract_*` trio, Local Renewal (cat 5) lacked `contract_*`, `helper_hkid_copy_*`, `release_letter_from_previous_employer_*`, `previous_contract_copy_*`, and Group 1/2 non-local categories other than 1 / 7 got the base 5 columns only — unpicked columns read as null, so the readiness gate (now shared by status change, status list and the save recompute) would have reported them missing forever and Ready to Submit would have been unreachable for those categories. Lists now match the field lists exactly (script-compared, all branches). (2) `contract-category-list` now returns `processing_group`, so the dashboard's Category filter really narrows to the Group (it silently showed all before). (3) `/invoices/list` takes `contract_id` and returns it; the Contract Details Invoice tab sends it (the client-side filter was a no-op because the row had no `contract_id`). (4) Dashboard per-user storage key reads `getCurrentUser().data.id` (was always "me"). (5) A CS Owner / CS Assistant column cannot stay hidden while its filter is active. (6) The restored scroll offset is cleared after use. (7) Contract panel "Save changes" sends the row version as last loaded (refreshed after each own autosave), not the one captured when Edit opened — otherwise a user's own checklist tick during Edit made Save answer 409. (8) Dead `isSkippedByImmigrationGate` import removed.
- Reason: two reviewers (frontend, backend) read the full diffs against the surrounding code. Checks after the fixes: admin tsc 0 errors; contract tsc 124 = baseline; `immigrationGate` / `applySignOffRules` / `sectionSignOffsToClear` 9/9 unit cases (unassessed, all not-apply, applies with missing rows, needs_review, base missing, physical original, section clear, Ready without Prepared, clearing Prepared); frontend ESLint clean; full esbuild bundle resolves.
- Status: Done — same restarts as 212–217 (admin, contract, processing, scheduler) + frontend rebuild. **Behaviour note:** an Immigration checklist that is Ready to Submit today without a recorded applicability assessment for its conditional sections will drop to Not Ready on its next save until the sections are assessed (EXTEND §5.2 — unknown is not No).

### 264 — frontend · Worker batch C · item 1: `version` on every record write, 409 STALE_VERSION handled (W-18 / W-23 / W-12)
- File: `src/redux/Slices/worker/WorkerSlice.js`, `src/redux/Slices/AddWorker.js` (rejections now carry `code` + `data`), `WorkerPage/worker-detail/WorkerDetail.js`, `WorkerPage/update-worker/EditWorker.js`, `WorkerPage/worker-detail/newui/details/WkdOtherContactCard.js`, `WorkerPage/worker-detail/profile-tab/biodata-tab/BiodataForm.js`, `WorkerPage/workerModuleConstants.js` (`STALE_VERSION_CODE`, `WORKER_STATUS_CHANGE_PAYLOAD.VERSION`).
- REQ-ID: `frontend-prompt-worker-C-246_2026-10-16.md` A.1; `worker_BACKEND_ANSWERS_C_2026-10-16.md` W-18 / W-23 / W-12 (admin API 246).
- Change: the detail's `version` travels on `update-worker-details` (Edit Worker, Other Contact), `save-worker-biodata` and `change-worker-status`. On 409 `STALE_VERSION` every surface toasts the API message and never retries: Edit Worker / Other Contact / Change Status reload the record (the dirty form keeps its edits — the baseline reset skips while dirty; the status confirm step stays open); Biodata keeps the open form and holds the version the 409 handed back for the next Save (re-reading the record there would overwrite the form).
- Status: Done — frontend. Pending: `worker_C_batch_2026-10-16.sql` + admin API / hrms restart on staging.

- Status: Done.

### 266 — frontend · Worker batch C · item 3: status change payload `{ worker_id, status, reason, version }`; In Review / Suspended never offered (W-12 / W-44)
- File: `WorkerPage/worker-detail/WorkerDetail.js`, `WorkerPage/workerModuleConstants.js` (`manualStatusTargets` now drops MANUAL_STATUS_NEVER_OFFERED — In Review 2, Suspended 5 — for the dashboard's global list; the Suspended gate key removed).

### 284 — frontend · Row action menus open every time (trigger captured before the state updater)
- File: `JobOfferPage/newui/record/JoApplicantList.js`, `Conversaction/newui/panels/GcJobOfferDetails.js`, `WorkerPage/dashboard/DashboardFilterBar.js`.
- REQ-ID: QA 2026-10-19 (Job Offer record → applicant list → Action): the menu opened on the first click only; later clicks showed nothing.
- Change: `openMenu` read `event.currentTarget` inside the `setMenu` updater. React may run that updater during the next render, after the synthetic event has been dispatched and its `currentTarget` reset to `null`; the menu then had no trigger, `JoActionMenu` skipped positioning and `.jor-menu[data-ready="false"]` kept it hidden. The trigger (and, on the Worker dashboard nationality menu, its bounding rect) is now read synchronously in the handler and the updater only toggles. Check: ESLint clean on the three files.
- Status: Done. Same fix applied to the identical menu in the Global Chat Job Offer panel and to the Worker dashboard nationality filter.

### 285 — frontend · Invoice header: Handling Staff / CS Owner locked until a Branch is chosen
- File: `Invoice/add-contract-invoice/CommonPageHeaderInvoice.js`, `Invoice/general/CommonPageHeaderInvoice.js`, `Invoice/new-ui-create.css`.
- REQ-ID: developer request 2026-10-19 (Add Contract Invoice screenshot): "Lock / blur the user dropdown until branch is selected; when the branch changes and the linked user has no access to the branch under the invoice module, reset the user dropdown; never allow the user dropdown to change if branch is not selected."
- Change: the CS select is `disabled` (muted, not-allowed cursor, dimmed chevron, tooltip "Please select branch first") while `branchId` is empty. Branch change keeps the existing behaviour — `searchStaff({ branch_id, type: 'cs' })` reloads the list and `reconcileCsWithList` clears a CS absent from it. New: clearing the branch back to "Please Select" no longer fires a staff search with an empty id; it clears `cs_id` / `csId` / `cs_name` and locks the select again. The general-invoice header (same Branch → CS pair, already resetting the CS on branch change) gets the same lock. Check: ESLint clean on both headers.
- Status: Done. Invoice module is otherwise another developer's — change limited to these two header files and two CSS rules, on the developer's explicit request.

### 286 — frontend · Invoice worker form: Early Release Date picker bounded by the Visa Expiry date
- File: `Invoice/add-contract-invoice/AddWorkerDetailsOfInvoice.js`.

- Change: the Early Release Date input carries `max` = the chosen Visa Expiry date minus one day (`watch("visa_expiry_date")`, `dayjs`), so the native picker greys out the expiry date and everything after it — the same rule `handleValidateDates` already enforces on change. No bound while Visa Expiry is blank. Check: ESLint clean.
- Status: Done. Invoice module is otherwise another developer's — change limited to this input, on the developer's explicit request.

### 287 — frontend · Invoice worker form: Contract Category can no longer save as a hidden stale value
- File: `Invoice/add-contract-invoice/AddWorkerDetailsOfInvoice.js`.
- REQ-ID: developer report 2026-10-19 (Add Contract Invoice → Worker details): Contract Category shows "Please Select" yet Next saves the record.
- Change: the category option list is fetched per Contract Status, but the form value `contract_category` kept the worker's stored id (or the id chosen under the previous status) even when the new list did not contain it. The select rendered its first option ("Please Select") for the unmatched value while yup's `required` passed on the stale id, so the record saved with a category the user never saw. A new effect reconciles the value with the list whenever the list changes: an id absent from the list is cleared (and the category-driven Wait-in-Macau rule reset), so the required check now fails until a category is picked. Check: ESLint — only the file's pre-existing warnings.
- Status: Done. Invoice module is otherwise another developer's — change limited to this effect, on the developer's explicit request.

### 288 — frontend · Invoice worker form: Early Release Date label carries the required star
- File: `Invoice/add-contract-invoice/AddWorkerDetailsOfInvoice.js`.

- Change: the label gets the same `req-star` span as the other required labels. The field only renders while Except Release Early is Yes, and it is mandatory whenever it renders, so the star is unconditional on the field. Check: ESLint — pre-existing warnings only.
- Status: Done.

### 289 — frontend · Invoice worker form: HKID red border clears as soon as the value is valid
- File: `Invoice/add-contract-invoice/{AddWorkerDetailsOfInvoice,AddEmployerDetailsOfInvoice}.js`, `Invoice/general/{AddWorkerDetailsOfInvoice,AddEmployerDetailsOfInvoice}.js`.
- REQ-ID: developer report 2026-10-19 (contract-invoice Worker HKID): after correcting an invalid HKID the red border stayed until submit. The same empty-message pattern sat in the Employer form of the flow and in both general-invoice forms (12 sites in all) and is fixed there too.
- Change: the HKID validator (and the submit re-check) "cleared" the error with `setError("hk_id", { message: "" })`; react-hook-form keeps the error object, so `errors.hk_id` stayed truthy and the `.invalid` class kept the red border while the message text was empty. Both sites now call `clearErrors("hk_id")`. Check: ESLint — pre-existing warnings only.
- Status: Done.

### 290 — frontend · Invoice edit modals: Back returns to the picker, Cancel closes the flow
- File: `Invoice/add-contract-invoice/{AddContractInvoice,AddWorkerDetailsOfInvoice,AddEmployerDetailsOfInvoice}.js`, `Invoice/general/{AddContractInvoice,AddWorkerDetailsOfInvoice,AddEmployerDetailsOfInvoice}.js`, `Invoice/exchange/selectContractExchange.js`.
- REQ-ID: developer report 2026-10-19 (Add Contract Invoice → Select Worker → edit worker): Back and Cancel both closed the edit modal; expected Back = one step (to Select Worker), Cancel = close everything; a first step shows Cancel only, Back exists from the second step on.
- Change: the four edit forms take an `onBack` prop; in modal mode Back (footer button and header arrow) calls it, Cancel keeps `onClose`. Back renders only when `onBack` is provided (or in route mode, where it stays `history.back()`), so a modal opened from the details card shows Cancel alone. The containers track whether the modal came from a picker (`empFormFromPicker` / `wkrFormFromPicker`): from Select Worker / New Employer → `onBack` closes the form and reopens that picker; from the card → no Back. Exchange has a worker picker only. Check: ESLint — no errors; warning counts unchanged from the committed files.
- Status: Done. Invoice module is otherwise another developer's — change made on the developer's explicit request.

### 291 — frontend · "New" Invoice / Insurance from a Worker or Employer record preselects that party
- File: `WorkerPage/worker-detail/newui/listing/{useListingParty (new),WorkerInvoiceListing,WorkerInsuranceListing}.js`, `insurance/new/NewInsuranceWizard.js`.

- Change: the shared Invoice and Insurance listing tabs (mounted by both record pages) build the party from the redux record that matches the page id (`useListingParty`) and pass it as navigation state. **Invoice:** `currentInvoiceWorker` / `currentInvoiceEmployer` — the keys both invoice create pages (contract and general) already seed from `location.state`, the same contract the Job Offer "New Contract Invoice" action uses — so the Worker / Employer card opens Selected. **Insurance:** `location.state.party` = `{ worker: { id, name }, employer: { id, name } }`; the wizard resolves each through the same search the picker dialogs run and matches by id, so the parties show the dialogs' facts; a Contract / prior entry (fixed parties) ignores it. Check: ESLint clean on the three worker-module files; wizard warnings unchanged from the committed file.
- Status: Done. Insurance module is otherwise another developer's — the wizard change is one effect + two imports, on the developer's explicit request.

Applies to every stage panel that renders the footer (Insurance, Consulate, Immigration, Visa, Deployment, Pre-Departure, Contract). Check: CSS only.

### 293 — frontend · Add Invoice draft survives a browser refresh (same tab)
- File: `redux/invoiceDraftSession.js` (new), `redux/store.js`.
- REQ-ID: product ruling 2026-10-19 (Shubham, chat): the in-progress Add Contract Invoice draft should be remembered across a refresh; cleared when the invoice is created or cancelled.
- Change: a store middleware mirrors the draft fields — `employer.currentInvoiceEmployerDetail`, `worker.currentInvoiceWorkerDetail`, `employer.currentInvoiceLocalInfo` (branch, CS owner, invoice date, Type (Internal)), `employer.invoiceCurrentJobInfo`, `invoices.invoiceHeaderForm`, `invoices.invoiceContractItemsList`, `invoices.invoiceContractDataState`, `invoices.invoiceItemsList` — to `sessionStorage["mm.invoiceDraft.v1"]` whenever one of them changes, and `hydrateInvoiceDraft(store)` dispatches them back through the slices' own setters at boot. Clearing needs no new code: Cancel, create success and arriving on the invoice list already clear these slices, and the mirror writes the emptied state (storage removed); logout clears session storage and the middleware also drops the key on `auth/logout/fulfilled`; the language-change store reset re-hydrates right after. Steps 2 / 3 keep reading their `location.state` (history state already survives a refresh), so with the redux draft back the items and the review render as before. sessionStorage, not redux-persist: tab lifetime only, no cross-session or cross-tab carry-over, the "server-data slices are never persisted" rule in store.js untouched. Covers the contract and general flows (same slices); the exchange flow's own `exchange*` fields are not mirrored. No Invoice page file changed. Check: ESLint clean on both files.
- Status: Done.

### 294 — frontend · Worker Details date fields: the calendar glyph opens a calendar

A pick emits the field's canonical value (`YYYY-MM-DD` / `YYYY-MM`) through the existing `onChange`, so every host's validation runs unchanged. Disabled fields keep the glyph disabled. No new i18n key: the trigger's label reuses `key_select_date` plus the field's own aria label. Check: ESLint clean on the component.
- Status: Done.

### 295 — frontend · Employer dashboard phone masking is a presentation rule on the client too
- File: `Employer/newui/{employerNewUiConstants,employerDashboardConfig,employerDashboardColumns}.js`, `__test__/employer/maskPhoneTail.test.js` (new).
- REQ-ID: ClickUp "Masking is a PRESENTATION rule, not a permission" (assigned 2026-10-21): applies uniformly to everyone who may view the dashboard, no administrator bypass, format `+852 •••• 1234` with the country code preserved, one bullet per hidden digit, exactly the last four exposed, em dash when missing, no reveal / hover / tooltip / title / aria label / copy path to the full number; Employer Details unaffected.
- Change: the dashboard printed Mobile / WhatsApp exactly as the server sent them (masked since admin API CHANGE_HISTORY 228). `maskPhoneTail` now guarantees the rendered text: every digit but the last four becomes U+2022, an already-masked value passes through unchanged, empty stays the em dash, the country code is prefixed as before. Applied to both dashboard phone columns only. The duplicates page stays unmasked and Contact-View-gated on purpose: sheet U-0041 (product recommendation, Mike to confirm) keeps full numbers there because comparing them is the screen's purpose, and DASH §6 scopes masking to the Dashboard — so the backend's planned masking of `/global/get-duplicate-employers` (Priyanka, 21 Oct) should wait for that ruling. Already true and unchanged: no title / tooltip / aria label / copy action on the cells, the row's accessible name is "Open Employer Details — Name · Code" only, Contact Details — View still decides whether the columns show at all (U-0030), the admin grant shortcut never unmasks. Employer Details untouched. Check: ESLint clean; new unit test (6 cases) passes.
- Status: Done — frontend; backend confirmed 2026-10-21 (Priyanka): `/get-employer-list` masks `mobile_number` / `whatsapp_number` server-side for every account type incl. admins, search is equality-only, `get-employer-detail` deliberately unmasked (DASH §6.5).

### 305 — frontend · Server failures (HTTP 5xx) show one plain message, never the raw server text
- File: `services/createApiInstance.js` (the one response interceptor every API client shares).
- REQ-ID: QA 2026-10-17 (Change CS Owner printed a raw Prisma query error in the toast); panel-wide hardening item.
- Change: on a 5xx response the interceptor rewrites the body in place — `message` becomes the existing `key_something_went_wrong` copy ("Something went wrong"; a hard fallback when i18n is not ready), the original text moves to `raw_message` and is logged to the console with the status and URL, `server_error: true` marks it, `status: false` keeps the `payload.status` readers on the error path. Readers are untouched: `showSuccessOrError` / `showErrorMessage` and every slice (`rejectWithValue(error.response)` or `(error.response.data)`) still read `message` where they did. 4xx bodies (validation, permission, the stable `code`s) pass through unchanged. The i18n helper is required lazily so the services layer never imports it at module load. Check: ESLint clean.
- Status: Done.

- Change: the repo tracks no package-lock, so every fresh install resolves react-bootstrap → @restart/ui → `@react-aria/ssr` to the newest 3.10.x (Apr / May 2026), which re-exports through `react-aria/…` sub-path exports that CRA 5's webpack cannot resolve. Any environment that installs from scratch (staging build) needs this pin.

### 327 — frontend · E-Visa panel: a changed Visa Expiry shows the Correction reason box and sends `reason`

- Change: the panel read the recorded Visa Expiry from the contract-level projection (`processingVisaDocumentModel.visa_expiry_date`) in read mode but seeded the edit draft from the visa document alone; on a record where the document lags the projection the client saw "no recorded value", hid the reason box, skipped its own check and sent no `reason`, so the server refused. One helper (`recordedExpiryOf`) now feeds the draft, the stored comparison, the textarea condition, the client validation and the payload. Rule kept: recorded value changed → reason required and sent; first record → none; Entry Visa Expiry unchanged (U-0284). Defensive: a `VISA_EXPIRY_CHANGE_REASON_REQUIRED` refusal (code, or the message as a fallback) opens the reason box and shows the message under it. The Contract Start guard was already client-side. Check: ESLint clean.
- Status: Done — frontend.

### 349 — frontend · Permissions save with version, 409 stale review, Copy source, change history (backend 263, ask P-4)
- File: `users/useUserForm.js`, `permissions/PermissionStaleDialog.js` (new), `permissions/PermissionHistoryDialog.js` (new), `permissions/permissionHistory.js` (new: key → control label, stored value → grid wording, grouping by version), `redux/Slices/settings/users/UsersSlice.js` (`fetchPermissionHistory`), `services/users.js`, `services/apiPath.js` (`/settings/permission/history`), `UserPermissionsEditor.js` (History button), `UserFormPage.js`.

- Change: the editor keeps the `version` read with the account's template and sends it on every save (not on create — the blank template carries none); a draft that is an unedited Copy saves with `source: "copy"`; the returned `version` replaces the stored one; `changed === 0` shows "No change…" instead of a success. A 409 `STALE_VERSION` keeps the draft and opens "Someone else changed this account's permissions": their `changed_since` rows (control label, old → new, actor, time) beside the admin's pending changes; Reload and review re-reads the account and re-applies the draft except on the keys the other save changed, then reopens Review; Discard my changes re-reads and drops the draft; the save is never retried automatically. History (Edit only, gate `view_user` today via one constant `PERM_HISTORY_GATE`): a dialog listing events newest first, grouped by version (time · actor · source save / copy / Migration / Eligibility · n changes), each row Control · Old → New in the grid's wording (branch ids → names from the template's `values`), Load more paging at 50. Check: ESLint; production build.

- Ask 1 — `POST /hrms/task/save` accepts `notes_from {template_id, version, lang en|zh}`: must point at a stored `task_template_versions` row (any template status) else 422 `notes_from`; stored in new nullable columns `tasks.notes_template_id / notes_template_version / notes_lang`; returned on every standard task object as `notes_from` (null when none). Omitted → untouched; `null` → cleared; a changed snapshot on edit counts as a "notes" edit in history.
- Ask 4 — `source_facts` on every task object (`loadSource` facts, request language via accept-language → `name_zh_HK` / `name_zh_CN` / `name_in` / `name_en`): job_offer `{employer_name, status}` (job_statuses), employer `{employer_name, location, chinese_name}` (locations), worker `{worker_name, status, nationality}` (worker_statuses, nationalities), insurance `{worker_name, employer_name, plan}` (price_lists.name via policy_id), contract `{employer_name, worker_name}`; invoice unchanged. Lookups are best-effort (a failed lookup gives null facts, never blocks the task).
- Ask 7 — `POST /hrms/task/templates/save` accepts `notes_en` / `notes_zh` (≤ 10000 chars; a present key sets that language on the NEW version, an absent key carries the current notes forward; create defaults to null).
- Ask 9 — notification routes: new `POST /hrms/notification/mark-read {notification_id}`, `POST /mark-as-unread {notification_id}` (the real unread), `POST /mark-all-as-read` (POST alias of the GET). Each single-row route touches only the caller's own row (404-style "Notification not found." as 400 otherwise), 422 without `notification_id`, and returns `{notification_id, is_read, total}` (new unread total). Legacy `POST /mark-unread` is UNCHANGED (it still marks as read — the current frontend relies on it) and marked deprecated in the route file.
- Ask 10 — A4 `GET /hrms/calendar/list` interview rows carry `stage_text`: `interview_bookings.job_stage_id → job_stages.status → job_stages_statuses` name in the request language (e.g. "Interview Invited"); null when the booking has no stage row. The existing `stage` (pending / upcoming / completed / cancelled list key) is untouched.
- Ask 11 — context data contract + validation on create: job_offer `applicant {id, code, name}` (id = **worker id**, must have a `job_stages` row on that Job Offer; `code` = worker reference, display only) and `interview {id, label}` (interview_bookings.id on that Job Offer); insurance `request {id, type, label}` (insurance_requests.id on that record) and `period {id, label}` (insurance_periods.id on that record) → 422 `context.<key>` when the row belongs elsewhere. On edit, `source_type` / `source_id` / `context` may only be echoed back unchanged (key-order-insensitive compare); a different value → 422 instead of being silently dropped. The current editor sends these on create only, so no frontend change is needed.
- Checks: hrms `tsc --noEmit` = 13 errors, all pre-existing (contract.invoice/category model `enum`, aws-sdk types, `Object.hasOwn`, app.ts ajv-formats) — none in the touched files; every new SQL statement parsed against the staging schema copy; the notes_from column SQL run twice on a scratch table (idempotent); helper unit checks (11/11) for `validateTaskInput` notes_from rules and the edit-time immutability compare.
