### 501 — frontend · Contract record · one lifecycle for the header and the stage panels (Mubin, contract 20; 3 Nov)
- Cause: the header read `contract_status` from the dashboard row, the stage panels read `status` from `GET /contract/global/details/:id`; a contract created through Add Contract / Invoice carried the two apart (header Processing, every panel locked for a fully permitted user).
- `processing-detail/contractLifecycle.js` (new): `ContractLifecycleContext`, `lifecycleOf(record, override)` (explicit value → `contract_status` → `status`), `useContractLifecycle` / `useIsProcessingContract`. `Contract/details/ProgressTab.js` provides `row.contract_status` around the terminal flow and the stage panel.
- The twelve stage panels (Contract, Consulate, Immigration, Insurance, Medical, Pre-Departure, Authorization Letter, E-Visa, DHL, Flight, HKID, Pick Up) and `ProcessingDetail.js`'s locked marker read the hook; `terminalFlowRules.isProcessingContract` / `getCompletingStatusIds` take the lifecycle id, passed by `ContractTerminalFlow`, `ProcessingDeploymentTab` and `useWithdrawnCompletingOptions`. Outside the provider nothing changes (the record's own fields decide, `contract_status` first).
- Backend side (Priyanka, sent 3 Nov): the create path must write `status` = 1 with `contract_status`; row 20 to be fixed.
- Test: `src/__test__/processing/contractLifecycle.test.js`.
- Status: Done — frontend; Mubin retests contract 20 after the staging rebuild.

### 502 — frontend · Contract record · a failed details read is an error, not a record (Priyanka, contract 20; 3 Nov)
- Priyanka's finding on contract 20: `contracts.status` and `contract_status` are one column (the Add Contract / Invoice path writes 1); the locked panels came from `GET /contract/global/details/20` failing to open the record and answering a **200 with no `status`**. The server now answers 404 with the reason; the data check on why 20 cannot be opened is hers.
- `redux/Slices/contract/contractExtraReducers.js`: a fulfilled details read with `status: false` or no `data`, and a rejected one (404 / network), both clear `contractDetails` (the previously opened contract no longer stays on screen) and keep the reason in the new `contractDetailsError` (`""` when the server gave none, `null` = no error, reset by a successful read). `ContractSlice.js`: the field in the initial state, the dedicated rejected handler.
- `Contract/details/ContractDetails.js`: the page now waits for both reads (details + dashboard row) before rendering; with `contractDetailsError` set it shows the error surface (`key_couldnt_load_this_view` + the server message, or `key_something_went_wrong`) on the neutral layout, checked before the no-row / Details-View surface. Existing keys only.
- CH 501 stays: the one-lifecycle context remains the right structure (header and panels can never disagree); its trigger here was the failed read.
- Test: `src/__test__/contract/contractDetailsReadError.test.js`.
- Status: Done — frontend; Mubin retests contract 20 after Priyanka's data check + the staging rebuild.

### 503 — frontend · Deployment panels · a document refresh no longer overwrites the input being typed in (Mubin, SIM Card Number; 4 Nov)
- Cause: each Deployment panel re-reads its draft from `deploymentDocument` after every save; the refresh of an earlier save (Sent On, Current Location …) could land while the user was typing in a blur-saved text input, undoing the keystrokes and backspaces since ("numbers not fully cleared").
- `deployment-tab/draftSync.js` (new) `withFocusedText(next, prev, fieldIds)`: the draft keeps the focused blur-saved input's text; `processingConstants.js` `DEPLOYMENT_TYPED_FIELDS` (input id → draft key: DHL `dhl-sim`, Flight `fl-no` / `fl-fr` / `fl-to`, HKID `hk-sp`). The three panels' refresh effects use it; Pick Up has no typed text.
- Not frontend — the digits change: "435465439999999222" comes back as "435465439999999200", which is exactly `Number("435465439999999222")` in JavaScript (and MySQL DOUBLE). The panel sends the SIM as a digit string; the API handles or stores it as a number → `docs/migration/priyanka_MESSAGE_2026-11-04.md` item 1 (store / return `sim_card_number` as a string; the 20-digit cap is the client rule).
- Test: `src/__test__/processing/deploymentDraftSync.test.js`.
- Backend 388 (Priyanka, 4 Nov 09:42): `sim_card_number` is text end to end (VARCHAR(20), `^[0-9]{0,20}$`, `""` clears, JSON string on read, a JSON number → 400). The panel already sends the input's string and `""` on clear — no client change; contract 35's cut value is re-entered after the deploy.
- Status: Done — frontend; retest after the admin deploy.
